```
1	//! Zero-copy no_std SPSC ring buffer over a caller-provided
2	//! memory region, per [notes/ring-buffer-design.md]:
3	//!
4	//! - One `#[repr(C)]` [`Header`] spanning four cache lines
5	//!   (immutable geometry, producer index, consumer index, app
6	//!   user words), followed by M slots of N bytes.
7	//! - Indices are free-running `AtomicU32`s, masked only at slot
8	//!   access; full is `p - c == M`, no sacrificial slot.
9	//! - Messages move in place: the producer writes through a
10	//!   [`WriteSlot`] `&mut T`, the consumer reads through a
11	//!   [`ReadSlot`] `&T` — zerocopy traits bound `T`, no
12	//!   serialization step. Each side reserves at most one slot at
13	//!   a time; the guard holds the endpoint borrow until commit /
14	//!   release (or drop).
15	//!
16	//! [notes/ring-buffer-design.md]: https://github.com/winksaville/zc-ring-x1/blob/main/notes/ring-buffer-design.md
17	
18	#![cfg_attr(not(test), no_std)]
19	
20	use core::marker::PhantomData;
21	use core::mem::{align_of, size_of};
22	use core::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering};
23	
24	mod consumer;
25	pub mod policy;
26	mod pool;
27	mod producer;
28	mod registry;
29	
30	pub use consumer::{Consumer, Empty, ReadSlot};
31	pub use pool::{BufSlot, Exhausted, Pool, PoolHeader, PoolResolver};
32	pub use producer::{Full, Producer, WriteSlot};
33	pub use registry::{Desc, PoolId, PoolRegistry, RegistryError};
34	
35	/// Cache-line size the layout is built around.
36	///
37	/// - Slot size must be a multiple of this; slots and the region
38	///   itself must be aligned to it.
39	pub const CACHE_LINE_SIZE: usize = 64;
40	
41	/// Layout marker written by [`Ring::init`]; rejects foreign
42	/// regions on attach.
43	const MAGIC: u32 = 0x5A43_5231; // "ZCR1"
44	
45	/// Bumped on any change to the region layout.
46	const LAYOUT_VERSION: u32 = 2;
47	
48	/// Number of `AtomicU32` words in the header's app-owned
49	/// `user` line.
50	pub const USER_WORDS: usize = 16;
51	
52	/// Cache-line-aligned wrapper granting its field sole
53	/// ownership of the line.
54	///
55	/// - `repr(align(N))` accepts only an integer literal — it
56	///   cannot name [`CACHE_LINE_SIZE`] — so the `64` is written out
57	///   and a const assert ties them back together.
58	#[repr(C, align(64))]
59	struct CacheAligned<T>(T);
60	
61	const _: () = assert!(align_of::<CacheAligned<AtomicU32>>() == CACHE_LINE_SIZE);
62	
63	impl<T> core::ops::Deref for CacheAligned<T> {
64	    type Target = T;
65	    /// Access the wrapped value.
66	    fn deref(&self) -> &T {
67	        &self.0
68	    }
69	}
70	
71	/// Control block at offset 0 of the region — one struct spanning
72	/// four cache lines so each concurrently-written index owns its
73	/// own line.
74	///
75	/// - line 0: geometry, written by [`Ring::init`] with `magic`
76	///   last (`Release`), read-only thereafter. Cold: per-op
77	///   paths use the handles' snapshots.
78	/// - line 1: `producer_idx`, written only by the producer.
79	/// - line 2: `consumer_idx`, written only by the consumer.
80	/// - line 3: `user`, app-owned scratch — last, so an app
81	///   overrun walks into its own slot data, not an index line.
82	/// - Every field is atomic: the region may be mapped by a peer
83	///   at any time, so even "immutable" fields must be free of
84	///   data races. `AtomicU32` has the same layout as `u32`, so
85	///   this does not affect `layout_version`.
86	#[repr(C)]
87	pub struct Header {
88	    /// Layout marker ([`MAGIC`]); stored last by init
89	    /// (`Release`), loaded first by attach (`Acquire`), so a
90	    /// peer that observes it also observes the geometry.
91	    magic: AtomicU32,
92	    /// Layout version ([`LAYOUT_VERSION`]).
93	    layout_version: AtomicU32,
94	    /// Slot size N in bytes — a [`CACHE_LINE_SIZE`] multiple.
95	    slot_size: AtomicU32,
96	    /// Slot count M — a power of two `<= 2^31`.
97	    capacity: AtomicU32,
98	    /// [`CACHE_LINE_SIZE`] this region was built with. The line size
99	    /// is a layout parameter (padding, offsets, slot granule),
100	    /// so builds must agree; attach validates it like the rest
101	    /// of the geometry.
102	    cache_line_size: AtomicU32,
103	    /// Free-running count of messages committed.
104	    producer_idx: CacheAligned<AtomicU32>,
105	    /// Free-running count of messages released.
106	    consumer_idx: CacheAligned<AtomicU32>,
107	    /// App-owned scratch line ([`USER_WORDS`] words): zeroed by
108	    /// init, then never read, written, or interpreted by the
109	    /// crate. See the design doc's "Blocking and user words".
110	    user: CacheAligned<[AtomicU32; USER_WORDS]>,
111	}
112	
113	const _: () = assert!(size_of::<Header>() == 4 * CACHE_LINE_SIZE);
114	
115	/// Errors from region validation — [`Ring::init`] /
116	/// [`Ring::attach`] and [`Pool::init`] / [`Pool::attach`].
117	#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
118	pub enum Error {
119	    /// Region is not [`CACHE_LINE_SIZE`]-aligned.
120	    Misaligned,
121	    /// Region is smaller than header + slots/buffers.
122	    TooSmall,
123	    /// Slot size is zero or not a [`CACHE_LINE_SIZE`] multiple.
124	    BadSlotSize,
125	    /// Capacity is zero, not a power of two, or `> 2^31`.
126	    BadCapacity,
127	    /// Pool: buffer size is zero or not a [`CACHE_LINE_SIZE`]
128	    /// multiple.
129	    BadBufSize,
130	    /// Pool: buffer count is zero or `u32::MAX` (the
131	    /// free-stack NIL sentinel).
132	    BadBufCount,
133	    /// Attach: magic mismatch — not a region of the expected
134	    /// kind.
135	    BadMagic,
136	    /// Attach: layout version mismatch.
137	    BadLayoutVersion,
138	    /// Attach: region built with a different [`CACHE_LINE_SIZE`].
139	    BadCacheLine,
140	}
141	
142	/// A validated view over a ring region; split into the two
143	/// endpoint handles with [`Ring::split`].
144	///
145	/// - Geometry is snapshotted out of the header at
146	///   init/attach: per-op paths never re-read fields a peer
147	///   could scribble on, so a hostile peer can degrade the ring
148	///   to garbage messages or spurious Full/Empty, never to UB.
149	pub struct Ring<'a> {
150	    /// The region's control block.
151	    header: &'a Header,
152	    /// Base of the slot array (offset `size_of::<Header>()`).
153	    slots: *mut u8,
154	    /// Snapshot of `header.slot_size`.
155	    slot_size: u32,
156	    /// Snapshot of `header.capacity`.
157	    capacity: u32,
158	    /// Slot-position mask (`capacity - 1`).
159	    mask: u32,
160	    _region: PhantomData<&'a [u8]>,
161	}
162	
163	impl<'a> Ring<'a> {
164	    /// Initialize a fresh region and return the ring over it.
165	    ///
166	    /// - `slot_size` — N bytes per slot, a [`CACHE_LINE_SIZE`]
167	    ///   multiple.
168	    /// - `capacity` — M slots, a power of two `<= 2^31`.
169	    /// - The region must be [`CACHE_LINE_SIZE`]-aligned and at least
170	    ///   `size_of::<Header>() + M * N` bytes.
171	    pub fn init(region: &'a mut [u8], slot_size: u32, capacity: u32) -> Result<Self, Error> {
172	        validate_geometry(slot_size, capacity)?;
173	        let len = region.len();
174	        // Taken exactly once: a second `as_mut_ptr()` would
175	        // retag the slice and invalidate `header` under Stacked
176	        // Borrows (Miri-verified).
177	        let base = region.as_mut_ptr();
178	        let header = header_ptr(base, len)?;
179	        if (len as u64) < region_size(slot_size, capacity) {
180	            return Err(Error::TooSmall);
181	        }
182	        // SAFETY: alignment + room for the Header checked by
183	        // header_ptr; region is exclusively borrowed for 'a;
184	        // any byte pattern is a valid Header (all-atomic
185	        // fields, plain-byte padding).
186	        let header = unsafe { &*header };
187	        header
188	            .layout_version
189	            .store(LAYOUT_VERSION, Ordering::Relaxed);
190	        header.slot_size.store(slot_size, Ordering::Relaxed);
191	        header.capacity.store(capacity, Ordering::Relaxed);
192	        header
193	            .cache_line_size
194	            .store(CACHE_LINE_SIZE as u32, Ordering::Relaxed);
195	        header.producer_idx.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
196	        header.consumer_idx.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
197	        for word in header.user.iter() {
198	            word.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
199	        }
200	        // Published last: a peer that pre-mapped the region must
201	        // never observe MAGIC before the geometry it validates.
202	        // (Re-initializing a region a peer is already attached
203	        // to is outside the contract.)
204	        header.magic.store(MAGIC, Ordering::Release);
205	        // SAFETY: in bounds — region.len() >= header + slots.
206	        let slots = unsafe { base.add(size_of::<Header>()) };
207	        Ok(Ring {
208	            header,
209	            slots,
210	            slot_size,
211	            capacity,
212	            mask: capacity - 1,
213	            _region: PhantomData,
214	        })
215	    }
216	
217	    /// Attach to a region another process (or an earlier call)
218	    /// already initialized, validating its header.
219	    ///
220	    /// # Safety
221	    ///
222	    /// - `region` points to `len` bytes of memory that outlive
223	    ///   `'a`, genuinely shared and writable (e.g. a
224	    ///   `MAP_SHARED` mapping).
225	    /// - No other producer attaches if this side will produce;
226	    ///   likewise for the consumer side (SPSC contract).
227	    pub unsafe fn attach(region: *mut u8, len: usize) -> Result<Self, Error> {
228	        let header = header_ptr(region, len)?;
229	        // SAFETY: alignment + room for the Header checked by
230	        // header_ptr; caller guarantees the memory is live and
231	        // shared.
232	        let header = unsafe { &*header };
233	        // Acquire pairs with init's Release store of magic: a
234	        // valid magic means the geometry stores are visible.
235	        if header.magic.load(Ordering::Acquire) != MAGIC {
236	            return Err(Error::BadMagic);
237	        }
238	        if header.layout_version.load(Ordering::Relaxed) != LAYOUT_VERSION {
239	            return Err(Error::BadLayoutVersion);
240	        }
241	        if header.cache_line_size.load(Ordering::Relaxed) != CACHE_LINE_SIZE as u32 {
242	            return Err(Error::BadCacheLine);
243	        }
244	        // Snapshot geometry once; per-op paths never re-read it.
245	        let slot_size = header.slot_size.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
246	        let capacity = header.capacity.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
247	        validate_geometry(slot_size, capacity)?;
248	        if (len as u64) < region_size(slot_size, capacity) {
249	            return Err(Error::TooSmall);
250	        }
251	        // SAFETY: in bounds — len >= header + slots.
252	        let slots = unsafe { region.add(size_of::<Header>()) };
253	        Ok(Ring {
254	            header,
255	            slots,
256	            slot_size,
257	            capacity,
258	            mask: capacity - 1,
259	            _region: PhantomData,
260	        })
261	    }
262	
263	    /// Split into the producer and consumer endpoint handles.
264	    ///
265	    /// - Consuming `self` makes each handle exist at most once
266	    ///   per ring per process; cross-process, one producing and
267	    ///   one consuming process is the SPSC contract.
268	    pub fn split(self) -> (Producer<'a>, Consumer<'a>) {
269	        (
270	            Producer::new(
271	                self.header,
272	                self.slots,
273	                self.slot_size,
274	                self.capacity,
275	                self.mask,
276	            ),
277	            Consumer::new(self.header, self.slots, self.slot_size, self.mask),
278	        )
279	    }
280	}
281	
282	/// Validate a region base pointer and cast it to the `Header`
283	/// it must start with; shared by [`Ring::init`] / [`Ring::attach`].
284	///
285	/// - Checks alignment and room for the `Header` itself. The
286	///   full-geometry length check stays with the caller: init
287	///   knows the geometry from its parameters, attach only after
288	///   reading it from the returned header.
289	/// - Returns a raw pointer — no deref here. Each caller
290	///   discharges its own safety obligation (init: exclusive
291	///   borrow; attach: the caller's shared-mapping contract).
292	fn header_ptr(base: *mut u8, len: usize) -> Result<*const Header, Error> {
293	    if !(base as usize).is_multiple_of(CACHE_LINE_SIZE) {
294	        return Err(Error::Misaligned);
295	    }
296	    if len < size_of::<Header>() {
297	        return Err(Error::TooSmall);
298	    }
299	    Ok(base as *const Header)
300	}
301	
302	/// Bytes needed for a region with the given geometry.
303	///
304	/// - Computed in u64: the usize product can wrap on 32-bit
305	///   targets (e.g. N = 2^26, M = 2^6), which would validate a
306	///   tiny region and let safe code write out of bounds.
307	fn region_size(slot_size: u32, capacity: u32) -> u64 {
308	    size_of::<Header>() as u64 + slot_size as u64 * capacity as u64
309	}
310	
311	/// Shared geometry checks for [`Ring::init`] / [`Ring::attach`].
312	fn validate_geometry(slot_size: u32, capacity: u32) -> Result<(), Error> {
313	    if slot_size == 0 || !(slot_size as usize).is_multiple_of(CACHE_LINE_SIZE) {
314	        return Err(Error::BadSlotSize);
315	    }
316	    // The `> 1 << 31` arm is unreachable (u32 powers of two are
317	    // <= 2^31); it stays to document the M bound the occupancy
318	    // math relies on.
319	    if capacity == 0 || !capacity.is_power_of_two() || capacity > 1 << 31 {
320	        return Err(Error::BadCapacity);
321	    }
322	    Ok(())
323	}
324	
325	/// Check `T` fits a slot; called once per `reserve_slot_with`
326	/// (both endpoints).
327	///
328	/// - Panics on a type-geometry mismatch — that is a programming
329	///   error, not a runtime condition.
330	fn check_type<T>(slot_size: u32) {
331	    assert!(size_of::<T>() <= slot_size as usize, "T larger than slot");
332	    assert!(
333	        core::mem::align_of::<T>() <= CACHE_LINE_SIZE,
334	        "T alignment exceeds slot alignment"
335	    );
336	}
337	
338	/// Non-panicking form of [`check_type`], for descriptor
339	/// resolve — there the pool compared against is selected by
340	/// untrusted input, which must not be able to select a panic.
341	fn type_fits<T>(slot_size: u32) -> bool {
342	    size_of::<T>() <= slot_size as usize && core::mem::align_of::<T>() <= CACHE_LINE_SIZE
343	}
344	
345	/// Pointer to the slot for free-running index `idx`.
346	///
347	/// - Returned range is `slot_size` bytes, cache-line aligned
348	///   (base is, and slot_size is a line multiple).
349	fn slot_ptr(slots: *mut u8, idx: u32, mask: u32, slot_size: u32) -> *mut u8 {
350	    let pos = (idx & mask) as usize;
351	    // SAFETY: pos < capacity, so the offset is inside the slot
352	    // array validated at init/attach.
353	    unsafe { slots.add(pos * slot_size as usize) }
354	}
355	
356	#[cfg(test)]
357	mod tests {
358	    use super::*;
359	    use zerocopy::{FromBytes, Immutable, IntoBytes, KnownLayout};
360	
361	    /// Test message; two words so a torn write would be visible.
362	    #[derive(FromBytes, IntoBytes, KnownLayout, Immutable, Debug, PartialEq)]
363	    #[repr(C)]
364	    struct Msg {
365	        seq: u64,
366	        val: u64,
367	    }
368	
369	    /// Test region size: header + (4 slots × 1 line each).
370	    const REGION_BYTES: usize = size_of::<Header>() + (4 * CACHE_LINE_SIZE);
371	
372	    /// Cache-line-aligned backing store for the tests' rings.
373	    #[repr(C, align(64))]
374	    struct Region([u8; REGION_BYTES]);
375	
376	    impl Region {
377	        fn new() -> Self {
378	            Region([0; REGION_BYTES])
379	        }
380	    }

382	    #[test]
383	    fn init_rejects_bad_geometry() {
384	        let mut r = Region::new();
385	        assert_eq!(
386	            Ring::init(&mut r.0, 63, 4).err().unwrap(),
387	            Error::BadSlotSize
388	        );
389	        assert_eq!(
390	            Ring::init(&mut r.0, 0, 4).err().unwrap(),
391	            Error::BadSlotSize
392	        );
393	        assert_eq!(
394	            Ring::init(&mut r.0, 64, 3).err().unwrap(),
395	            Error::BadCapacity
396	        );
397	        assert_eq!(
398	            Ring::init(&mut r.0, 64, 0).err().unwrap(),
399	            Error::BadCapacity
400	        );
401	        assert_eq!(Ring::init(&mut r.0, 64, 8).err().unwrap(), Error::TooSmall);
402	        assert_eq!(
403	            Ring::init(&mut r.0[1..], 64, 4).err().unwrap(),
404	            Error::Misaligned
405	        );
406	        // 32-bit tripwire: N * M wraps a 32-bit usize (2^26 *
407	        // 2^6 = 2^32); the u64 region_size must still reject it.
408	        assert_eq!(
409	            Ring::init(&mut r.0, 1 << 26, 1 << 6).err().unwrap(),
410	            Error::TooSmall
411	        );
412	    }
413	
414	    #[test]
415	    fn attach_validates_header() {
416	        let mut r = Region::new();
417	        // Not initialized yet: magic is zero.
418	        let err = unsafe { Ring::attach(r.0.as_mut_ptr(), r.0.len()) }
419	            .err()
420	            .unwrap();
421	        assert_eq!(err, Error::BadMagic);
422	        Ring::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap();
423	        let ring = unsafe { Ring::attach(r.0.as_mut_ptr(), r.0.len()) }.unwrap();
424	        assert_eq!(ring.slot_size, 64);
425	        assert_eq!(ring.capacity, 4);
426	        assert_eq!(ring.mask, 3);
427	        // A region built with a different CACHE_LINE_SIZE (simulated
428	        // by editing the recorded value) is rejected.
429	        ring.header.cache_line_size.store(128, Ordering::Relaxed);
430	        let err = unsafe { Ring::attach(r.0.as_mut_ptr(), r.0.len()) }
431	            .err()
432	            .unwrap();
433	        assert_eq!(err, Error::BadCacheLine);
434	    }
435	
436	    #[test]
437	    fn user_words_zeroed_and_shared() {
438	        let mut r = Region::new();
439	        // Dirty the region so init's zeroing is observable.
440	        r.0.fill(0xAA);
441	        let ring = Ring::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap();
442	        let (prod, mut cons) = ring.split();
443	        assert!(prod.user().iter().all(|w| w.load(Ordering::Relaxed) == 0));
444	        // Both endpoints see the same line; a producer-side
445	        // store is visible through the consumer's accessor.
446	        prod.user()[0].store(7, Ordering::Release);
447	        assert_eq!(cons.user()[0].load(Ordering::Acquire), 7);
448	        // The user line must not alias ring state: scribbling
449	        // all of it leaves the ring empty and functional.
450	        for w in cons.user().iter() {
451	            w.store(u32::MAX, Ordering::Relaxed);
452	        }
453	        assert!(cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).is_err());
454	    }
455	
456	    #[test]
457	    fn indices_survive_u32_wrap() {
458	        let mut r = Region::new();
459	        let ring = Ring::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap();
460	        // Simulate a long-running ring two commits shy of the
461	        // u32 wrap (empty state: p == c).
462	        let start = u32::MAX - 1;
463	        ring.header.producer_idx.store(start, Ordering::Relaxed);
464	        ring.header.consumer_idx.store(start, Ordering::Relaxed);
465	        let (mut prod, mut cons) = ring.split();
466	
467	        // Fill: producer_idx runs MAX-1, MAX, 0, 1 — masked
468	        // slot positions 2, 3, 0, 1.
469	        for i in 0..4u64 {
470	            let mut slot = prod.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();
471	            slot.seq = i;
472	            slot.val = i;
473	            slot.commit();
474	        }
475	        assert!(prod.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).is_err());
476	
477	        // Drain in order across the wrap.
478	        for i in 0..4u64 {
479	            let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();
480	            assert_eq!(msg.seq, i);
481	            msg.release();
482	        }
483	        assert!(cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).is_err());
484	    }
485	
486	    #[test]
487	    fn roundtrip_full_empty() {
488	        let mut r = Region::new();
489	        let (mut prod, mut cons) = Ring::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap().split();
490	
491	        // Empty at start.
492	        assert!(cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).is_err());
493	
494	        // Fill all 4 slots, then Full.
495	        for i in 0..4u64 {
496	            let mut slot = prod.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();
497	            slot.seq = i;
498	            slot.val = i * 10;
499	            slot.commit();
500	        }
501	        assert!(prod.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).is_err());
502	
503	        // Drain in order; wrap: slot 0 is reused by seq 4.
504	        for i in 0..4u64 {
505	            let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();
506	            assert_eq!(
507	                *msg,
508	                Msg {
509	                    seq: i,
510	                    val: i * 10
511	                }
512	            );
513	            msg.release();
514	        }
515	        assert!(cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).is_err());
516	
517	        // One more write lands in the masked-around slot 0.
518	        let mut slot = prod.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();
519	        slot.seq = 4;
520	        slot.commit();
521	        let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();
522	        assert_eq!(msg.seq, 4);
523	        msg.release();
524	    }
525	
526	    #[test]
527	    // Dropping the guards is the behavior under test; they have
528	    // no Drop impl by design (abandon = do nothing).
529	    #[allow(clippy::drop_non_drop)]
530	    fn abandoned_guards_publish_nothing() {
531	        let mut r = Region::new();
532	        let (mut prod, mut cons) = Ring::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap().split();
533	
534	        // Reserve then drop: nothing published.
535	        let mut slot = prod.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();
536	        slot.seq = 99;
537	        drop(slot);
538	        assert!(cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).is_err());
539	
540	        // Commit one, reserve then drop: still unread, the
541	        // next reservation returns it again.
542	        let mut slot = prod.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();
543	        slot.seq = 1;
544	        slot.commit();
545	        let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();
546	        assert_eq!(msg.seq, 1);
547	        drop(msg);
548	        let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();
549	        assert_eq!(msg.seq, 1);
550	        msg.release();
551	    }

553	    #[test]
554	    fn threaded_spsc() {
555	        // Reduced under Miri: interpreted spin loops are slow,
556	        // and its scheduler explores interleavings at any count.
557	        const COUNT: u64 = if cfg!(miri) { 200 } else { 100_000 };
558	        let mut r = Region::new();
559	        let (mut prod, mut cons) = Ring::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap().split();
560	
561	        std::thread::scope(|s| {
562	            s.spawn(move || {
563	                for i in 0..COUNT {
564	                    loop {
565	                        match prod.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false) {
566	                            Ok(mut slot) => {
567	                                slot.seq = i;
568	                                slot.val = i * 3;
569	                                slot.commit();
570	                                break;
571	                            }
572	                            Err(Full) => std::hint::spin_loop(),
573	                        }
574	                    }
575	                }
576	            });
577	            s.spawn(move || {
578	                for i in 0..COUNT {
579	                    loop {
580	                        match cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false) {
581	                            Ok(msg) => {
582	                                assert_eq!(msg.seq, i);
583	                                assert_eq!(msg.val, i * 3);
584	                                msg.release();
585	                                break;
586	                            }
587	                            Err(Empty) => std::hint::spin_loop(),
588	                        }
589	                    }
590	                }
591	            });
592	        });
593	    }
594	
595	    #[test]
596	    fn reserve_slot_with_policy_counts_and_gives_up() {
597	        let mut r = Region::new();
598	        let (mut prod, mut cons) = Ring::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap().split();
599	
600	        // Empty ring: the consumer's policy sees 0-based
601	        // attempts and its `false` becomes Err(Empty).
602	        let mut seen = Vec::new();
603	        let err = cons
604	            .reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|attempt| {
605	                seen.push(attempt);
606	                attempt < 2
607	            })
608	            .err()
609	            .unwrap();
610	        assert_eq!(err, Empty);
611	        assert_eq!(seen, [0, 1, 2]);
612	
613	        // Fill the ring: the producer's policy gives up with
614	        // Err(Full).
615	        for i in 0..4 {
616	            let mut slot = prod.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();
617	            slot.seq = i;
618	            slot.commit();
619	        }
620	        let err = prod
621	            .reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|attempt| attempt < 2)
622	            .err()
623	            .unwrap();
624	        assert_eq!(err, Full);
625	
626	        // With room / messages available the policy is never
627	        // consulted.
628	        let msg = cons
629	            .reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| panic!("policy consulted with a message available"))
630	            .unwrap();
631	        assert_eq!(msg.seq, 0);
632	        msg.release();
633	        let mut slot = prod
634	            .reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| panic!("policy consulted with room available"))
635	            .unwrap();
636	        slot.seq = 4;
637	        slot.commit();
638	    }
639

641	    fn threaded_spsc_spin_variants() {
642	        // The threaded_spsc flow through the shipped
643	        // policy::spin on both ends — the shipped-policy model
644	        // in use.
645	        const COUNT: u64 = if cfg!(miri) { 200 } else { 100_000 };
646	        let mut r = Region::new();
647	        let (mut prod, mut cons) = Ring::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap().split();
648	
649	        std::thread::scope(|s| {

## Per-commit flow

Every commit (Preparation, each Work commit, Close-out) goes
through:

1. **Mark this commit `(current)`** as the first edit in
   `notes/todo.md > ## In Progress`.
2. **Do the work** (see [Iterative work](#iterative-work)
   for the loop-and-squash technique).
3. **Flip this commit `(current)` → `(done)`** in `## In
   Progress` — before the cargo cycle and the commit.
4. **Validate the artifact** — a medium-specific step, skip-able
   for notes-only commits, mandatory at close-out. For the Rust
   example the cargo cycle is:
   1. `cargo fmt`
   2. `cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings`
   3. `cargo test`
   4. `cargo install --path . --locked`
   5. (re-test if anything substantive changed)
5. **Work review.** Stop *before* writing any description;
   tell the user "ready to commit." The user reviews the
   changes and we iterate until complete.
6. **Write the commit description** — see
   [Commit description](#commit-description).
7. **Commit Description review.** Show the title + body
   and stop. The user reviews the description. Iterate.
8. **Commit.** `jj commit -m "title" -m "body" -R .` for
   the app repo, `-R .claude` for the bot repo (`-R` last
   keeps the verb visible):

   ```
   jj commit -m \
   "<type>: <short description>" \
   -m "<intro paragraph>

   - file1: gist
   - file2: gist

   ochid: /.claude/<chid>" \
   -R .
   ```

**Two overrides apply:**

- **Deviation or question** — any time the work deviates
  from the agreed plan, or a question arises, stop and
  surface it; don't push through.
- **ESC-ESC** — the user can interrupt at any point to pull
  a review or question forward.

## Commit description
```

1	//! Consuming endpoint: [`Consumer`] reserves the oldest unread
2	//! slot, the [`ReadSlot`] guard reads it in place and releases.
3	
4	use core::marker::PhantomData;
5	use core::ops::Deref;
6	use core::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering};
7	use zerocopy::{FromBytes, Immutable, KnownLayout};
8	
9	use crate::{Header, USER_WORDS, check_type, slot_ptr};
10	
11	/// `reserve_slot_with` failed: no unread messages.
12	#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
13	pub struct Empty;
14	
15	/// The consuming endpoint: `reserve_slot_with` the oldest
16	/// unread slot, read in place, `release`.
17	pub struct Consumer<'a> {
18	    /// The ring's control block.
19	    header: &'a Header,
20	    /// Base of the slot array.
21	    slots: *mut u8,
22	    /// Geometry snapshot (see [`Ring`](crate::Ring)).
23	    slot_size: u32,
24	    /// Slot-position mask (`capacity - 1`).
25	    mask: u32,
26	    _region: PhantomData<&'a [u8]>,
27	}
28	
29	// SAFETY: the handle owns the consumer role; see the Producer
30	// Send rationale.
31	unsafe impl Send for Consumer<'_> {}
32	
33	impl<'a> Consumer<'a> {
34	    /// Build the handle from [`Ring::split`](crate::Ring::split)'s
35	    /// geometry snapshot.
36	    pub(crate) fn new(header: &'a Header, slots: *mut u8, slot_size: u32, mask: u32) -> Self {
37	        Consumer {
38	            header,
39	            slots,
40	            slot_size,
41	            mask,
42	            _region: PhantomData,
43	        }
44	    }
45	
46	    /// The header's app-owned scratch line: zeroed at init,
47	    /// never touched by the crate again.
48	    ///
49	    /// - Shared with the peer: treat contents as untrusted
50	    ///   data — store values, never addresses to dereference.
51	    /// - See the design doc's "Blocking and user words" for
52	    ///   the wakeup-protocol contract it exists to host.
53	    pub fn user(&self) -> &[AtomicU32; USER_WORDS] {
54	        &self.header.user
55	    }
56	
57	    /// Reserve the oldest unread slot as a `&T`, applying an
58	    /// injected wait policy: retry until a message arrives or
59	    /// the policy gives up → [`Empty`].
60	    ///
61	    /// - Only one slot may be reserved at a time: the guard
62	    ///   holds the `&mut Consumer` borrow, so a second
63	    ///   reservation before the guard is dropped or released
64	    ///   does not compile.
65	    /// - Dropping the guard without [`ReadSlot::release`]
66	    ///   leaves the slot unread — the next reservation
67	    ///   returns it again.
68	    /// - `on_empty` is called after each failed attempt with
69	    ///   the attempt count (0-based, saturating); returning
70	    ///   `false` gives up → `Err(Empty)`. Pass `|_| false`
71	    ///   for a single non-blocking probe.
72	    /// - The wait loop is the reservation itself (the guard
73	    ///   borrows the endpoint, so retrying could not return
74	    ///   it): `consumer_idx` is ours and loaded once; only
75	    ///   `producer_idx` is re-read per attempt.
76	    /// - See [`policy`](crate::policy) for shipped policies
77	    ///   and the composition model.
78	    pub fn reserve_slot_with<T>(
79	        &mut self,
80	        mut on_empty: impl FnMut(u32) -> bool,
81	    ) -> Result<ReadSlot<'_, T>, Empty>
82	    where
83	        T: FromBytes + KnownLayout + Immutable,
84	    {
85	        check_type::<T>(self.slot_size);
86	        // Ours alone: no peer moves it, load once.
87	        let c = self.header.consumer_idx.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
88	        let mut attempt = 0u32;
89	        loop {
90	            let p = self.header.producer_idx.load(Ordering::Acquire);
91	            if p != c {
92	                break;
93	            }
94	            if !on_empty(attempt) {
95	                return Err(Empty);
96	            }
97	            attempt = attempt.saturating_add(1);
98	        }
99	        // Raw pointer, not `&T`: release's store frees the slot
100	        // for producer writes while a reference field would
101	        // still be argument-protected. Deref mints short-lived
102	        // references instead.
103	        let msg = slot_ptr(self.slots, c, self.mask, self.slot_size) as *const T;
104	        Ok(ReadSlot {
105	            header: self.header,
106	            msg,
107	            next_idx: c.wrapping_add(1),
108	            _slot: PhantomData,
109	        })
110	    }
111	}
112	
113	/// A reserved read slot: `Deref` to read the message, then
114	/// [`release`](ReadSlot::release).
115	pub struct ReadSlot<'c, T> {
116	    /// The ring's control block (for the release store).
117	    header: &'c Header,
118	    /// The slot, viewed as the message type. Raw on purpose —
119	    /// see the comment in [`Consumer::reserve_slot_with`].
120	    msg: *const T,
121	    /// Value `consumer_idx` takes on release.
122	    next_idx: u32,
123	    /// Owns the `&'c` borrow of the consumer.
124	    _slot: PhantomData<&'c T>,
125	}
126	
127	impl<T> Deref for ReadSlot<'_, T> {
128	    type Target = T;
129	    /// Read access to the in-slot message.
130	    fn deref(&self) -> &T {
131	        // SAFETY: msg is in-bounds and aligned (check_type +
132	        // cache-line slot base), any byte pattern is a valid T
133	        // (FromBytes bound at reserve_slot_with), and the index
134	        // protocol gives this guard read access until release.
135	        unsafe { &*self.msg }
136	    }
137	}
138	
139	impl<T> ReadSlot<'_, T> {
140	    /// Free the slot for reuse (`consumer_idx + 1`, `Release`).
141	    pub fn release(self) {
142	        self.header
143	            .consumer_idx
144	            .store(self.next_idx, Ordering::Release);
145	    }
146	}
147	
```

{

  "content": "//! MPSC ring: multi-producer single-consumer sibling of the\n//! SPSC ring, per the design doc's \"MPSC ring (sibling\n//! primitive)\" section:\n//!\n//! - Same region shape as the SPSC ring — a four-line\n//!   [`MpscHeader`] then slots — plus a per-slot sequence\n//!   array between them: `seq[pos]` publishes each slot\n//!   independently, because claim order and commit order\n//!   differ under concurrent producers.\n//! - Producers CAS-claim `producer_idx`; the consumer stays\n//!   CAS-free. Fullness is read from the slot seq, so\n//!   producers never load `consumer_idx`.\n//! - Own magic and layout version: a region is one kind or\n//!   the other, and cross-attaching fails toward\n//!   [`Error::BadMagic`].\n//! - The claim CAS raises the atomic floor for MPSC users\n//!   only — this module is gated on `target_has_atomic =\n//!   \"32\"`; the SPSC ring stays load/store-only.\n\nuse core::marker::PhantomData;\nuse core::mem::size_of;\nuse core::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering};\n\nuse crate::{CACHE_LINE_SIZE, CacheAligned, Error, USER_WORDS};\n\n/// Layout marker written by [`MpscRing::init`]; distinct from\n/// the SPSC magic so cross-kind attach fails toward\n/// [`Error::BadMagic`].\nconst MPSC_MAGIC: u32 = 0x5A43_4D31; // \"ZCM1\"\n\n/// Bumped on any change to the MPSC region layout;\n/// independent of the SPSC layout version.\nconst MPSC_LAYOUT_VERSION: u32 = 1;\n\n/// Capacity bound: `2^30`, not the SPSC `2^31` — the seq\n/// tombstone encoding reserves index-arithmetic headroom (see\n/// the design doc's \"MPSC open questions\").\nconst MPSC_MAX_CAPACITY: u32 = 1 << 30;\n\n/// Control block at offset 0 of an MPSC region — same\n/// four-line shape as the SPSC [`Header`](crate::Header), but\n/// its own type: the layouts evolve independently and the\n/// index-ownership story differs.\n///\n/// - line 0: geometry, written by [`MpscRing::init`] with\n///   `magic` last (`Release`), read-only thereafter.\n/// - line 1: `producer_idx` — CAS-claimed by *all* producers\n///   (the contended line), never read by the consumer's hot\n///   path.\n/// - line 2: `consumer_idx` — written only by the consumer;\n///   never read by producers (fullness comes from the slot\n///   seq), so it is resume state and diagnostic occupancy.\n/// - line 3: `user`, app-owned scratch — same contract as the\n///   SPSC user line.\n/// - Every field is atomic: the region may be mapped by a\n///   peer at any time.\n#[repr(C)]\npub struct MpscHeader {\n    /// Layout marker ([`MPSC_MAGIC`]); stored last by init\n    /// (`Release`), loaded first by attach (`Acquire`).\n    magic: AtomicU32,\n    /// Layout version ([`MPSC_LAYOUT_VERSION`]).\n    layout_version: AtomicU32,\n    /// Slot size N in bytes — a [`CACHE_LINE_SIZE`] multiple.\n    slot_size: AtomicU32,\n    /// Slot count M — a power of two `<= 2^30`.\n    capacity: AtomicU32,\n    /// [`CACHE_LINE_SIZE`] this region was built with.\n    cache_line_size: AtomicU32,\n    /// Free-running count of slot positions claimed by\n    /// producers (CAS).\n    producer_idx: CacheAligned<AtomicU32>,\n    /// Free-running count of messages released; consumer-owned\n    /// resume state, not read by producers.\n    consumer_idx: CacheAligned<AtomicU32>,\n    /// App-owned scratch line ([`USER_WORDS`] words): zeroed by\n    /// init, then never touched by the crate.\n    user: CacheAligned<[AtomicU32; USER_WORDS]>,\n}\n\nconst _: () = assert!(size_of::<MpscHeader>() == 4 * CACHE_LINE_SIZE);\n\n/// A validated view over an MPSC ring region.\n///\n/// - Geometry is snapshotted out of the header at init/attach,\n///   as the SPSC ring does: per-op paths never re-read fields\n///   a peer could scribble on.\n/// - Region layout: [`MpscHeader`], the seq array (`M ×\n///   AtomicU32`, padded up to a cache line), then M slots of N\n///   bytes.\n// Fields feed the endpoint handles landing in the next commit\n// (`split`, send_with/recv); the allow goes with it.\n#[allow(dead_code)]\npub struct MpscRing<'a> {\n    /// The region's control block.\n    header: &'a MpscHeader,\n    /// Base of the per-slot sequence array.\n    seqs: *const AtomicU32,\n    /// Base of the slot array.\n    slots: *mut u8,\n    /// Snapshot of `header.slot_size`.\n    slot_size: u32,\n    /// Snapshot of `header.capacity`.\n    capacity: u32,\n    /// Slot-position mask (`capacity - 1`).\n    mask: u32,\n    _region: PhantomData<&'a [u8]>,\n}\n\nimpl<'a> MpscRing<'a> {\n    /// Initialize a fresh region and return the ring over it.\n    ///\n    /// - `slot_size` — N bytes per slot, a [`CACHE_LINE_SIZE`]\n    ///   multiple.\n    /// - `capacity` — M slots, a power of two `<= 2^30`.\n    /// - The region must be [`CACHE_LINE_SIZE`]-aligned and at\n    ///   least [`mpsc_region_size`] bytes.\n    pub fn init(region: &'a mut [u8], slot_size: u32, capacity: u32) -> Result<Self, Error> {\n        validate_mpsc_geometry(slot_size, capacity)?;\n        let len = region.len();\n        // Taken exactly once — see `Ring::init` for the Stacked\n        // Borrows rationale.\n        let base = region.as_mut_ptr();\n        let header = mpsc_header_ptr(base, len)?;\n        if (len as u64) < mpsc_region_size(slot_size, capacity) {\n            return Err(Error::TooSmall);\n        }\n        // SAFETY: alignment + room for the MpscHeader checked\n        // by mpsc_header_ptr; region is exclusively borrowed\n        // for 'a; any byte pattern is a valid MpscHeader\n        // (all-atomic fields, plain-byte padding).\n        let header = unsafe { &*header };\n        header\n            .layout_version\n            .store(MPSC_LAYOUT_VERSION, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        header.slot_size.store(slot_size, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        header.capacity.store(capacity, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        header\n            .cache_line_size\n            .store(CACHE_LINE_SIZE as u32, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        header.producer_idx.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        header.consumer_idx.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        for word in header.user.iter() {\n            word.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        }\n        // SAFETY: in bounds — len covers header + seq array.\n        let seqs = unsafe { base.add(size_of::<MpscHeader>()) } as *const AtomicU32;\n        // seq[i] = i marks every slot claimable by the position\n        // that will first reach it.\n        for i in 0..capacity {\n            // SAFETY: i < capacity, inside the seq array\n            // validated by the region-size check.\n            unsafe { &*seqs.add(i as usize) }.store(i, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        }\n        // Published last: a peer that pre-mapped the region must\n        // never observe the magic before the geometry and seq\n        // stores it validates and relies on.\n        header.magic.store(MPSC_MAGIC, Ordering::Release);\n        // SAFETY: in bounds — len covers header + seqs + slots.\n        let slots = unsafe { base.add(slots_offset(capacity)) };\n        Ok(MpscRing {\n            header,\n            seqs,\n            slots,\n            slot_size,\n            capacity,\n            mask: capacity - 1,\n            _region: PhantomData,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Attach to a region another process (or an earlier call)\n    /// already initialized, validating its header.\n    ///\n    /// # Safety\n    ///\n    /// - `region` points to `len` bytes of memory that outlive\n    ///   `'a`, genuinely shared and writable (e.g. a\n    ///   `MAP_SHARED` mapping).\n    /// - At most one consumer attaches; any number of\n    ///   producers may (MPSC contract).\n    pub unsafe fn attach(region: *mut u8, len: usize) -> Result<Self, Error> {\n        let header = mpsc_header_ptr(region, len)?;\n        // SAFETY: alignment + room for the MpscHeader checked\n        // by mpsc_header_ptr; caller guarantees the memory is\n        // live and shared.\n        let header = unsafe { &*header };\n        // Acquire pairs with init's Release store of magic: a\n        // valid magic means the geometry and seq stores are\n        // visible.\n        if header.magic.load(Ordering::Acquire) != MPSC_MAGIC {\n            return Err(Error::BadMagic);\n        }\n        if header.layout_version.load(Ordering::Relaxed) != MPSC_LAYOUT_VERSION {\n            return Err(Error::BadLayoutVersion);\n        }\n        if header.cache_line_size.load(Ordering::Relaxed) != CACHE_LINE_SIZE as u32 {\n            return Err(Error::BadCacheLine);\n        }\n        // Snapshot geometry once; per-op paths never re-read it.\n        let slot_size = header.slot_size.load(Ordering::Relaxed);\n        let capacity = header.capacity.load(Ordering::Relaxed);\n        validate_mpsc_geometry(slot_size, capacity)?;\n        if (len as u64) < mpsc_region_size(slot_size, capacity) {\n            return Err(Error::TooSmall);\n        }\n        // SAFETY: in bounds — len covers header + seqs + slots.\n        let seqs = unsafe { region.add(size_of::<MpscHeader>()) } as *const AtomicU32;\n        // SAFETY: as above.\n        let slots = unsafe { region.add(slots_offset(capacity)) };\n        Ok(MpscRing {\n            header,\n            seqs,\n            slots,\n            slot_size,\n            capacity,\n            mask: capacity - 1,\n            _region: PhantomData,\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/// Validate a region base pointer and cast it to the\n/// [`MpscHeader`] it must start with; shared by init / attach.\n///\n/// - Same split of duties as the SPSC `header_ptr`: alignment\n///   and header room here, full-geometry length with the\n///   caller.\nfn mpsc_header_ptr(base: *mut u8, len: usize) -> Result<*const MpscHeader, Error> {\n    if !(base as usize).is_multiple_of(CACHE_LINE_SIZE) {\n        return Err(Error::Misaligned);\n    }\n    if len < size_of::<MpscHeader>() {\n        return Err(Error::TooSmall);\n    }\n    Ok(base as *const MpscHeader)\n}\n\n/// Bytes the seq array occupies, padded up to a cache line so\n/// the slot array behind it stays line-aligned.\n///\n/// - u64: `capacity * 4` reaches `2^32` at the `2^30` cap,\n///   which would wrap a 32-bit usize.\nfn seq_bytes(capacity: u32) -> u64 {\n    (capacity as u64 * size_of::<AtomicU32>() as u64).next_multiple_of(CACHE_LINE_SIZE as u64)\n}\n\n/// Byte offset of the slot array: header, then the padded seq\n/// array.\n///\n/// - usize return: callers offset a pointer with it, and only\n///   after the u64 region-size check has proven the region —\n///   hence this offset — fits in memory.\nfn slots_offset(capacity: u32) -> usize {\n    size_of::<MpscHeader>() + seq_bytes(capacity) as usize\n}\n\n/// Bytes needed for an MPSC region with the given geometry.\n///\n/// - Computed in u64 for the same 32-bit wrap reason as the\n///   SPSC `region_size`.\npub fn mpsc_region_size(slot_size: u32, capacity: u32) -> u64 {\n    size_of::<MpscHeader>() as u64 + seq_bytes(capacity) + slot_size as u64 * capacity as u64\n}\n\n/// Geometry checks for [`MpscRing::init`] / [`MpscRing::attach`].\n///\n/// - Slot size as the SPSC ring; capacity additionally capped\n///   at [`MPSC_MAX_CAPACITY`] for tombstone headroom.\nfn validate_mpsc_geometry(slot_size: u32, capacity: u32) -> Result<(), Error> {\n    if slot_size == 0 || !(slot_size as usize).is_multiple_of(CACHE_LINE_SIZE) {\n        return Err(Error::BadSlotSize);\n    }\n    if capacity == 0 || !capacity.is_power_of_two() || capacity > MPSC_MAX_CAPACITY {\n        return Err(Error::BadCapacity);\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::Ring;\n\n    /// Test region: header + seq line (4 × 4 B padded to 64) +\n    /// 4 slots × 1 line.\n    const REGION_BYTES: usize = size_of::<MpscHeader>() + CACHE_LINE_SIZE + (4 * CACHE_LINE_SIZE);\n\n    /// Cache-line-aligned backing store for the tests' rings.\n    #[repr(C, align(64))]\n    struct Region([u8; REGION_BYTES]);\n\n    impl Region {\n        fn new() -> Self {\n            Region([0; REGION_BYTES])\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn mpsc_region_size_accounts_for_seq_array() {\n        // 4 slots: header 256 + seq 16→64 + slots 256 = 576.\n        assert_eq!(mpsc_region_size(64, 4), 576);\n        // 32 slots: seq 128 is already a line multiple.\n        assert_eq!(\n            mpsc_region_size(64, 32),\n            (size_of::<MpscHeader>() + 128 + 32 * 64) as u64\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn mpsc_init_rejects_bad_geometry() {\n        let mut r = Region::new();\n        assert_eq!(\n            MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 63, 4).err().unwrap(),\n            Error::BadSlotSize\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 0, 4).err().unwrap(),\n            Error::BadSlotSize\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 3).err().unwrap(),\n            Error::BadCapacity\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 0).err().unwrap(),\n            Error::BadCapacity\n        );\n        // Over the MPSC cap (fine for SPSC): tombstone headroom.\n        assert_eq!(\n            MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 1 << 31).err().unwrap(),\n            Error::BadCapacity\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 8).err().unwrap(),\n            Error::TooSmall\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            MpscRing::init(&mut r.0[1..], 64, 4).err().unwrap(),\n            Error::Misaligned\n        );\n        // 32-bit tripwire, as the SPSC test: N * M wraps a\n        // 32-bit usize; the u64 region size must still reject.\n        assert_eq!(\n            MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 1 << 26, 1 << 6).err().unwrap(),\n            Error::TooSmall\n        );\n        // An SPSC-sized region (no seq line) is too small here.\n        let spsc_bytes = size_of::<crate::Header>() + 4 * 64;\n        assert_eq!(\n            MpscRing::init(&mut r.0[..spsc_bytes], 64, 4).err().unwrap(),\n            Error::TooSmall\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn mpsc_init_seeds_seq_array() {\n        let mut r = Region::new();\n        // Dirty the region so the seq stores are observable.\n        r.0.fill(0xAA);\n        let ring = MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap();\n        for i in 0..4 {\n            // SAFETY: i < capacity; test mirrors init's bounds.\n            let seq = unsafe { &*ring.seqs.add(i as usize) };\n            assert_eq!(seq.load(Ordering::Relaxed), i);\n        }\n        assert_eq!(ring.slot_size, 64);\n        assert_eq!(ring.capacity, 4);\n        assert_eq!(ring.mask, 3);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn mpsc_attach_validates_header() {\n        let mut r = Region::new();\n        // Not initialized yet: magic is zero.\n        let err = unsafe { MpscRing::attach(r.0.as_mut_ptr(), r.0.len()) }\n            .err()\n            .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(err, Error::BadMagic);\n        MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap();\n        let ring = unsafe { MpscRing::attach(r.0.as_mut_ptr(), r.0.len()) }.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(ring.slot_size, 64);\n        assert_eq!(ring.capacity, 4);\n        // A different recorded cache line is rejected.\n        ring.header.cache_line_size.store(128, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        let err = unsafe { MpscRing::attach(r.0.as_mut_ptr(), r.0.len()) }\n            .err()\n            .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(err, Error::BadCacheLine);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn cross_kind_attach_fails_bad_magic() {\n        // An SPSC-initialized region is not an MPSC region…\n        let mut r = Region::new();\n        Ring::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap();\n        let err = unsafe { MpscRing::attach(r.0.as_mut_ptr(), r.0.len()) }\n            .err()\n            .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(err, Error::BadMagic);\n        // …and an MPSC-initialized region is not an SPSC one.\n        let mut r = Region::new();\n        MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap();\n        let err = unsafe { Ring::attach(r.0.as_mut_ptr(), r.0.len()) }\n            .err()\n            .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(err, Error::BadMagic);\n    }\n}\n"
}

```
//! MPSC ring: multi-producer single-consumer sibling of the
//! SPSC ring, per the design doc's "MPSC ring (sibling
//! primitive)" section:
//!
//! - Same region shape as the SPSC ring — a four-line
//!   [`MpscHeader`] then slots — plus a per-slot sequence
//!   array between them: `seq[pos]` publishes each slot
//!   independently, because claim order and commit order
//!   differ under concurrent producers.
//! - Producers CAS-claim `producer_idx`; the consumer stays
//!   CAS-free. Fullness is read from the slot seq, so
//!   producers never load `consumer_idx`.
//! - Own magic and layout version: a region is one kind or
//!   the other, and cross-attaching fails toward
//!   [`Error::BadMagic`].
//! - The claim CAS raises the atomic floor for MPSC users
//!   only — this module is gated on `target_has_atomic =
//!   "32"`; the SPSC ring stays load/store-only.

use core::marker::PhantomData;
use core::mem::size_of;
use core::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering};

use crate::{CACHE_LINE_SIZE, CacheAligned, Error, USER_WORDS};

/// Layout marker written by [`MpscRing::init`]; distinct from
/// the SPSC magic so cross-kind attach fails toward
/// [`Error::BadMagic`].
const MPSC_MAGIC: u32 = 0x5A43_4D31; // "ZCM1"
```

  "new_string": "use crate::{CACHE_LINE_SIZE, CacheAligned, Error, USER_WORDS};\n\nmod consumer;\nmod producer;\n\npub use consumer::{MpscConsumer, MpscReadSlot};\npub use producer::MpscProducer;\n\n/// Layout marker written by [`MpscRing::init`]; distinct from"
}
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/zc-ring-x1/src/mpsc/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Capacity bound: `2^30`, not the SPSC `2^31` — the seq\n/// tombstone encoding reserves index-arithmetic headroom (see\n/// the design doc's \"MPSC open questions\").\nconst MPSC_MAX_CAPACITY: u32 = 1 << 30;",
  "new_string": "/// Capacity bound: `2^30`, not the SPSC `2^31` — the seq\n/// tombstone encoding reserves index-arithmetic headroom (see\n/// the design doc's \"MPSC open questions\").\nconst MPSC_MAX_CAPACITY: u32 = 1 << 30;\n\n/// Tombstone offset: an unwound `send_with` commits\n/// `pos + 1 + TOMBSTONE` instead of `pos + 1`, marking a\n/// claimed slot the consumer must release without delivering.\n///\n/// - Unambiguous at both wait points: the consumer at `c`\n///   legitimately sees only `seq - (c+1)` of `-1` (not yet\n///   committed) or `0` (committed), so exactly `2^31` means\n///   tombstone; a producer's wrapping-signed diff reads a\n///   tombstoned previous lap as negative — \"full\" — until the\n///   consumer skips it, which is the correct backpressure.\npub(crate) const TOMBSTONE: u32 = 1 << 31;"
}
```

  "new_string": "            mask: capacity - 1,\n            _region: PhantomData,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Split into one producer handle and the consumer handle.\n    ///\n    /// - The producer is `Clone`: one clone per producing\n    ///   thread (MPSC contract). The consumer is unique per\n    ///   process; cross-process, exactly one consuming process\n    ///   is the caller's contract (see [`MpscRing::attach`]).\n    pub fn split(self) -> (MpscProducer<'a>, MpscConsumer<'a>) {\n        (\n            MpscProducer::new(\n                self.header,\n                self.seqs,\n                self.slots,\n                self.slot_size,\n                self.mask,\n            ),\n            MpscConsumer::new(\n                self.header,\n                self.seqs,\n                self.slots,\n                self.slot_size,\n                self.capacity,\n                self.mask,\n            ),\n        )\n    }\n}\n\n/// Validate a region base pointer and cast it to the\n/// [`MpscHeader`] it must start with; shared by init / attach."
}

  "content": "//! MPSC producing endpoint: [`MpscProducer`] claims a slot\n//! position by CAS, a closure fills it in place, and the\n//! commit happens on closure return — abandonment is\n//! unrepresentable (see the design doc's \"MPSC API: closure\n//! send\").\n\nuse core::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering};\nuse zerocopy::{FromBytes, IntoBytes, KnownLayout};\n\nuse super::{MpscHeader, TOMBSTONE};\nuse crate::{Full, USER_WORDS, check_type, slot_ptr};\n\n/// A producing handle: `Clone` one per producing thread, then\n/// `send_with`.\n///\n/// - `send_with` takes `&self` — exclusivity comes from the\n///   claim CAS, not the borrow, so one handle may also be\n///   shared by reference.\npub struct MpscProducer<'a> {\n    /// The ring's control block.\n    header: &'a MpscHeader,\n    /// Base of the per-slot sequence array.\n    seqs: *const AtomicU32,\n    /// Base of the slot array.\n    slots: *mut u8,\n    /// Geometry snapshot (see [`MpscRing`](super::MpscRing)).\n    slot_size: u32,\n    /// Slot-position mask (`capacity - 1`).\n    mask: u32,\n}\n\nimpl Clone for MpscProducer<'_> {\n    /// A second producing handle over the same ring; the claim\n    /// CAS serializes them.\n    fn clone(&self) -> Self {\n        MpscProducer { ..*self }\n    }\n}\n\n// SAFETY: any number of producers is the protocol contract —\n// the shared state (producer_idx, seqs) is atomic, slot claims\n// are exclusive by CAS, and slot writes are handed off with\n// Release/Acquire ordering.\nunsafe impl Send for MpscProducer<'_> {}\n// SAFETY: send_with is &self and every access is protected as\n// above, so shared references across threads are equally fine.\nunsafe impl Sync for MpscProducer<'_> {}\n\n/// Commits-on-drop guard armed while the fill closure runs: a\n/// panic unwinding through `send_with` must not abandon the\n/// claimed position (the consumer would wait there forever),\n/// so drop-without-disarm publishes the tombstoned commit the\n/// consumer releases without delivering.\nstruct TombstoneOnUnwind<'s> {\n    /// The claimed slot's seq word.\n    seq: &'s AtomicU32,\n    /// The commit value (`pos + 1`).\n    commit: u32,\n}\n\nimpl Drop for TombstoneOnUnwind<'_> {\n    /// Unwind path only — the normal path disarms with\n    /// `mem::forget`.\n    fn drop(&mut self) {\n        // Release: the (garbage) slot bytes must still be a\n        // well-defined handoff; the consumer skips the message\n        // but touches the seq protocol state.\n        self.seq\n            .store(self.commit.wrapping_add(TOMBSTONE), Ordering::Release);\n    }\n}\n\nimpl<'a> MpscProducer<'a> {\n    /// Build the handle from [`MpscRing::split`](super::MpscRing::split)'s\n    /// geometry snapshot.\n    pub(super) fn new(\n        header: &'a MpscHeader,\n        seqs: *const AtomicU32,\n        slots: *mut u8,\n        slot_size: u32,\n        mask: u32,\n    ) -> Self {\n        MpscProducer {\n            header,\n            seqs,\n            slots,\n            slot_size,\n            mask,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// The header's app-owned scratch line — same contract as\n    /// the SPSC endpoints' `user()`.\n    pub fn user(&self) -> &[AtomicU32; USER_WORDS] {\n        &self.header.user\n    }\n\n    /// The seq word for free-running position `idx`.\n    fn seq(&self, idx: u32) -> &AtomicU32 {\n        // SAFETY: idx is masked to < capacity, inside the seq\n        // array validated at init/attach.\n        unsafe { &*self.seqs.add((idx & self.mask) as usize) }\n    }\n\n    /// Claim the next slot position, fill it in place, commit\n    /// on closure return; retry a full ring under the injected\n    /// wait policy → [`Full`].\n    ///\n    /// - `fill` writes the message through `&mut T`; commit is\n    ///   by construction — there is no abandonment state (an\n    ///   SPSC-style guard could be dropped, and in MPSC an\n    ///   abandoned *claim* wedges the queue).\n    /// - `on_full` is called after each failed attempt with\n    ///   the attempt count (0-based, saturating); returning\n    ///   `false` gives up → `Err(Full)`. Pass `|_| false` for\n    ///   a single non-blocking probe. A send that returns\n    ///   `Full` has zero protocol footprint (the overflow-FIFO\n    ///   seam — see the design doc's \"Overflow readiness\").\n    /// - Losing a claim race to another producer is *not* a\n    ///   policy call: the system made progress; this producer\n    ///   just retries with the fresher index.\n    /// - If `fill` panics, the unwind publishes a tombstoned\n    ///   commit: the consumer releases the slot without\n    ///   delivering it, and the queue keeps flowing.\n    pub fn send_with<T>(\n        &self,\n        mut on_full: impl FnMut(u32) -> bool,\n        fill: impl FnOnce(&mut T),\n    ) -> Result<(), Full>\n    where\n        T: FromBytes + IntoBytes + KnownLayout,\n    {\n        check_type::<T>(self.slot_size);\n        let mut pos = self.header.producer_idx.load(Ordering::Relaxed);\n        let mut attempt = 0u32;\n        loop {\n            // Acquire pairs with the consumer's Release in\n            // release(): a claimable seq means the previous\n            // lap's reads are done.\n            let seq = self.seq(pos).load(Ordering::Acquire);\n            let diff = seq.wrapping_sub(pos) as i32;\n            if diff == 0 {\n                // Claimable. Weak CAS: a spurious failure just\n                // retries with the fresher index.\n                match self.header.producer_idx.compare_exchange_weak(\n                    pos,\n                    pos.wrapping_add(1),\n                    Ordering::Relaxed,\n                    Ordering::Relaxed,\n                ) {\n                    Ok(_) => break,\n                    Err(actual) => pos = actual,\n                }\n            } else if diff < 0 {\n                // Slot's previous occupant not yet released.\n                // Re-read the index first: if it moved, this\n                // was a stale pos (e.g. a tombstoned slot we\n                // raced), not a full ring — no policy call.\n                let cur = self.header.producer_idx.load(Ordering::Relaxed);\n                if cur != pos {\n                    pos = cur;\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if !on_full(attempt) {\n                    return Err(Full);\n                }\n                attempt = attempt.saturating_add(1);\n            } else {\n                // Another producer claimed pos; ours is stale.\n                pos = self.header.producer_idx.load(Ordering::Relaxed);\n            }\n        }\n        // pos is exclusively ours until the seq commit store.\n        let msg = slot_ptr(self.slots, pos, self.mask, self.slot_size) as *mut T;\n        let commit = pos.wrapping_add(1);\n        let guard = TombstoneOnUnwind {\n            seq: self.seq(pos),\n            commit,\n        };\n        // SAFETY: msg is in-bounds and aligned (check_type +\n        // cache-line slot base), any byte pattern is a valid T\n        // (FromBytes bound), and the claim CAS gives exclusive\n        // slot access until the commit store below.\n        fill(unsafe { &mut *msg });\n        // Normal path: disarm the unwind guard, then publish.\n        let seq = guard.seq;\n        core::mem::forget(guard);\n        // Release pairs with the consumer's Acquire seq load:\n        // observing pos + 1 means the filled bytes are visible.\n        seq.store(commit, Ordering::Release);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/zc-ring-x1/src/mpsc/consumer.rs",
  "content": "//! MPSC consuming endpoint: [`MpscConsumer`] reserves the\n//! oldest committed slot through the [`MpscReadSlot`] guard —\n//! the SPSC consumer's shape, plus tombstone skipping (an\n//! unwound producer's slot is released without delivery).\n\nuse core::marker::PhantomData;\nuse core::ops::Deref;\nuse core::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering};\nuse zerocopy::{FromBytes, Immutable, KnownLayout};\n\nuse super::{MpscHeader, TOMBSTONE};\nuse crate::{Empty, USER_WORDS, check_type, slot_ptr};\n\n/// The consuming handle: single per ring, CAS-free —\n/// `reserve_slot_with` the oldest committed slot, read in\n/// place, `release`.\npub struct MpscConsumer<'a> {\n    /// The ring's control block.\n    header: &'a MpscHeader,\n    /// Base of the per-slot sequence array.\n    seqs: *const AtomicU32,\n    /// Base of the slot array.\n    slots: *mut u8,\n    /// Geometry snapshot (see [`MpscRing`](super::MpscRing)).\n    slot_size: u32,\n    /// Geometry snapshot; release stores `pos + capacity`.\n    capacity: u32,\n    /// Slot-position mask (`capacity - 1`).\n    mask: u32,\n    _region: PhantomData<&'a [u8]>,\n}\n\n// SAFETY: the handle owns the single-consumer role; shared\n// state (seqs, indices) is atomic with Release/Acquire\n// handoff.\nunsafe impl Send for MpscConsumer<'_> {}\n\nimpl<'a> MpscConsumer<'a> {\n    /// Build the handle from [`MpscRing::split`](super::MpscRing::split)'s\n    /// geometry snapshot.\n    pub(super) fn new(\n        header: &'a MpscHeader,\n        seqs: *const AtomicU32,\n        slots: *mut u8,\n        slot_size: u32,\n        capacity: u32,\n        mask: u32,\n    ) -> Self {\n        MpscConsumer {\n            header,\n            seqs,\n            slots,\n            slot_size,\n            capacity,\n            mask,\n            _region: PhantomData,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// The header's app-owned scratch line — same contract as\n    /// the SPSC endpoints' `user()`.\n    pub fn user(&self) -> &[AtomicU32; USER_WORDS] {\n        &self.header.user\n    }\n\n    /// The seq word for free-running position `idx`.\n    fn seq(&self, idx: u32) -> &AtomicU32 {\n        // SAFETY: idx is masked to < capacity, inside the seq\n        // array validated at init/attach.\n        unsafe { &*self.seqs.add((idx & self.mask) as usize) }\n    }\n\n    /// Reserve the oldest committed slot as a `&T`, applying\n    /// an injected wait policy: retry until a message arrives\n    /// or the policy gives up → [`Empty`].\n    ///\n    /// - The guard behaves as the SPSC [`ReadSlot`]\n    ///   (crate::ReadSlot): drop without\n    ///   [`MpscReadSlot::release`] re-delivers the same slot.\n    /// - Tombstoned slots (a producer panicked mid-fill) are\n    ///   released and skipped inline; skipping is progress,\n    ///   not an attempt, so the policy is not consulted for\n    ///   them.\n    /// - `on_empty` is called after each failed attempt with\n    ///   the attempt count (0-based, saturating); returning\n    ///   `false` gives up → `Err(Empty)`. Pass `|_| false`\n    ///   for a single non-blocking probe.\n    pub fn reserve_slot_with<T>(\n        &mut self,\n        mut on_empty: impl FnMut(u32) -> bool,\n    ) -> Result<MpscReadSlot<'_, T>, Empty>\n    where\n        T: FromBytes + KnownLayout + Immutable,\n    {\n        check_type::<T>(self.slot_size);\n        let mut attempt = 0u32;\n        // Ours alone: producers never read it, only we store it.\n        let mut c = self.header.consumer_idx.load(Ordering::Relaxed);\n        loop {\n            let expected = c.wrapping_add(1);\n            // Acquire pairs with the producer's Release commit:\n            // observing expected means the fill is visible.\n            let seq = self.seq(c).load(Ordering::Acquire);\n            let diff = seq.wrapping_sub(expected);\n            if diff == 0 {\n                break;\n            }\n            if diff == TOMBSTONE {\n                // Unwound producer: release the slot without\n                // delivering and move on.\n                self.header.consumer_idx.store(expected, Ordering::Relaxed);\n                self.seq(c)\n                    .store(c.wrapping_add(self.capacity), Ordering::Release);\n                c = expected;\n                continue;\n            }\n            // Not committed yet (or a peer-corrupted seq —\n            // degrade toward Empty, never toward reading an\n            // unowned slot).\n            if !on_empty(attempt) {\n                return Err(Empty);\n            }\n            attempt = attempt.saturating_add(1);\n        }\n        // Raw pointer, not `&T` — same argument-protector\n        // rationale as the SPSC ReadSlot.\n        let msg = slot_ptr(self.slots, c, self.mask, self.slot_size) as *const T;\n        Ok(MpscReadSlot {\n            header: self.header,\n            seq: self.seq(c),\n            msg,\n            next_idx: c.wrapping_add(1),\n            released_seq: c.wrapping_add(self.capacity),\n            _slot: PhantomData,\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/// A reserved read slot: `Deref` to read the message, then\n/// [`release`](MpscReadSlot::release).\npub struct MpscReadSlot<'c, T> {\n    /// The ring's control block (for the consumer_idx store).\n    header: &'c MpscHeader,\n    /// The reserved slot's seq word (for the release store).\n    seq: &'c AtomicU32,\n    /// The slot, viewed as the message type. Raw on purpose —\n    /// see the SPSC `ReadSlot`.\n    msg: *const T,\n    /// Value `consumer_idx` takes on release.\n    next_idx: u32,\n    /// Value the seq takes on release (`pos + capacity`):\n    /// claimable again one lap later.\n    released_seq: u32,\n    /// Owns the `&'c` borrow of the consumer.\n    _slot: PhantomData<&'c T>,\n}\n\nimpl<T> Deref for MpscReadSlot<'_, T> {\n    type Target = T;\n    /// Read access to the in-slot message.\n    fn deref(&self) -> &T {\n        // SAFETY: msg is in-bounds and aligned (check_type +\n        // cache-line slot base), any byte pattern is a valid T\n        // (FromBytes bound), and the seq protocol gives this\n        // guard read access until release.\n        unsafe { &*self.msg }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl<T> MpscReadSlot<'_, T> {\n    /// Free the slot for reuse.\n    ///\n    /// - `consumer_idx` first (`Relaxed` — consumer-private\n    ///   resume state, racing readers of it don't exist), the\n    ///   seq store last (`Release` — the protocol-visible\n    ///   handoff producers acquire). A crash between the two\n    ///   is outside the contract, as any torn-state crash is.\n    pub fn release(self) {\n        self.header\n            .consumer_idx\n            .store(self.next_idx, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        self.seq.store(self.released_seq, Ordering::Release);\n    }\n}\n"
}

r.0, 64, 4).unwrap();\n        let err = unsafe { MpscRing::attach(r.0.as_mut_ptr(), r.0.len()) }\n            .err()\n            .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(err, Error::BadMagic);\n        // …and an MPSC-initialized region is not an SPSC one.\n        let mut r = Region::new();\n        MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap();\n        let err = unsafe { Ring::attach(r.0.as_mut_ptr(), r.0.len()) }\n            .err()\n            .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(err, Error::BadMagic);\n    }\n\n    use crate::{Empty, Full};\n    use zerocopy::{FromBytes, Immutable, IntoBytes, KnownLayout};\n\n    /// Test message; two words so a torn write would be\n    /// visible, `val` doubles as the producer id in threaded\n    /// tests.\n    #[derive(FromBytes, IntoBytes, KnownLayout, Immutable, Debug, PartialEq)]\n    #[repr(C)]\n    struct Msg {\n        seq: u64,\n        val: u64,\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn mpsc_roundtrip_full_empty() {\n        let mut r = Region::new();\n        let (prod, mut cons) = MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap().split();\n\n        // Empty at start.\n        assert!(cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).is_err());\n\n        // Fill all 4 slots, then Full on a single probe.\n        for i in 0..4u64 {\n            prod.send_with::<Msg>(|_| false, |m| {\n                m.seq = i;\n                m.val = i * 10;\n            })\n            .unwrap();\n        }\n        assert_eq!(\n            prod.send_with::<Msg>(|_| false, |_| {}).err().unwrap(),\n            Full\n        );\n\n        // Drain in order; then empty again.\n        for i in 0..4u64 {\n            let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();\n            assert_eq!(\n                *msg,\n                Msg {\n                    seq: i,\n                    val: i * 10\n                }\n            );\n            msg.release();\n        }\n        assert!(cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).is_err());\n\n        // One more send lands in the masked-around slot 0.\n        prod.send_with::<Msg>(|_| false, |m| {\n            m.seq = 4;\n            m.val = 0;\n        })\n        .unwrap();\n        let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(msg.seq, 4);\n        msg.release();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn mpsc_policies_count_and_give_up() {\n        let mut r = Region::new();\n        let (prod, mut cons) = MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap().split();\n\n        // Empty ring: the consumer's policy sees 0-based\n        // attempts and its `false` becomes Err(Empty).\n        let mut seen = Vec::new();\n        let err = cons\n            .reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|attempt| {\n                seen.push(attempt);\n                attempt < 2\n            })\n            .err()\n            .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(err, Empty);\n        assert_eq!(seen, [0, 1, 2]);\n\n        // Fill the ring: the producer's policy gives up with\n        // Err(Full), and the failed send has zero footprint —\n        // the fill closure never ran.\n        for i in 0..4u64 {\n            prod.send_with::<Msg>(|_| false, |m| m.seq = i).unwrap();\n        }\n        let mut seen = Vec::new();\n        let err = prod\n            .send_with::<Msg>(\n                |attempt| {\n                    seen.push(attempt);\n                    attempt < 2\n                },\n                |_| panic!(\"fill ran on a full ring\"),\n            )\n            .err()\n            .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(err, Full);\n        assert_eq!(seen, [0, 1, 2]);\n\n        // With room / messages available the policy is never\n        // consulted.\n        let msg = cons\n            .reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| panic!(\"policy consulted with a message available\"))\n            .unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(msg.seq, 0);\n        msg.release();\n        prod.send_with::<Msg>(\n            |_| panic!(\"policy consulted with room available\"),\n            |m| m.seq = 4,\n        )\n        .unwrap();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    // Dropping the guard is the behavior under test.\n    #[allow(clippy::drop_non_drop)]\n    fn mpsc_abandoned_read_guard_redelivers() {\n        let mut r = Region::new();\n        let (prod, mut cons) = MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap().split();\n\n        prod.send_with::<Msg>(|_| false, |m| m.seq = 1).unwrap();\n        let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(msg.seq, 1);\n        drop(msg);\n        let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(msg.seq, 1);\n        msg.release();\n        assert!(cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn mpsc_panicking_fill_tombstones_and_skips() {\n        let mut r = Region::new();\n        let (prod, mut cons) = MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap().split();\n\n        // A committed message, a panicked fill, another\n        // committed message.\n        prod.send_with::<Msg>(|_| false, |m| m.seq = 1).unwrap();\n        let unwound = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {\n            prod.send_with::<Msg>(|_| false, |m| {\n                m.seq = 99; // partially filled, then…\n                panic!(\"fill panics\");\n            })\n        }));\n        assert!(unwound.is_err());\n        prod.send_with::<Msg>(|_| false, |m| m.seq = 2).unwrap();\n\n        // The consumer sees 1 then 2; the tombstoned slot is\n        // released without delivery.\n        let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(msg.seq, 1);\n        msg.release();\n        let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(msg.seq, 2);\n        msg.release();\n        assert!(cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).is_err());\n\n        // The skipped slot is claimable again: a full lap\n        // still fits 4 messages.\n        for i in 3..7u64 {\n            prod.send_with::<Msg>(|_| false, |m| m.seq = i).unwrap();\n        }\n        assert!(prod.send_with::<Msg>(|_| false, |_| {}).is_err());\n        for i in 3..7u64 {\n            let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();\n            assert_eq!(msg.seq, i);\n            msg.release();\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn mpsc_indices_survive_u32_wrap() {\n        let mut r = Region::new();\n        let ring = MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap();\n        // Simulate a long-running ring two commits shy of the\n        // u32 wrap: indices at MAX-1, and each slot's seq must\n        // equal the free-running position that will next claim\n        // it (positions MAX-1, MAX, 0, 1 → slots 2, 3, 0, 1).\n        let start = u32::MAX - 1;\n        ring.header.producer_idx.store(start, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        ring.header.consumer_idx.store(start, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        for (slot, pos) in [(2u32, u32::MAX - 1), (3, u32::MAX), (0, 0), (1, 1)] {\n            // SAFETY: slot < capacity; test mirrors init.\n            unsafe { &*ring.seqs.add(slot as usize) }.store(pos, Ordering::Relaxed);\n        }\n        let (prod, mut cons) = ring.split();\n\n        // Fill across the wrap, then Full.\n        for i in 0..4u64 {\n            prod.send_with::<Msg>(|_| false, |m| m.seq = i).unwrap();\n        }\n        assert!(prod.send_with::<Msg>(|_| false, |_| {}).is_err());\n\n        // Drain in order across the wrap.\n        for i in 0..4u64 {\n            let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).unwrap();\n            assert_eq!(msg.seq, i);\n            msg.release();\n        }\n        assert!(cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn threaded_mpsc_two_producers() {\n        // Reduced under Miri: interpreted spin loops are slow.\n        const COUNT: u64 = if cfg!(miri) { 100 } else { 50_000 };\n        const PRODUCERS: u64 = 2;\n        let mut r = Region::new();\n        let (prod, mut cons) = MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap().split();\n\n        std::thread::scope(|s| {\n            for p in 0..PRODUCERS {\n                let prod = prod.clone();\n                s.spawn(move || {\n                    for i in 0..COUNT {\n                        prod.send_with::<Msg>(crate::policy::spin, |m| {\n                            m.seq = i;\n                            m.val = p;\n                        })\n                        .unwrap(); // OK: policy::spin never gives up\n                    }\n                });\n            }\n            s.spawn(move || {\n                // Global arrival order is claim order; only\n                // per-producer FIFO is promised.\n                let mut next = [0u64; PRODUCERS as usize];\n                for _ in 0..PRODUCERS * COUNT {\n                    let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(crate::policy::spin).unwrap(); // OK: policy::spin never gives up\n                    let p = msg.val as usize;\n                    assert_eq!(msg.seq, next[p], \"per-producer order broken\");\n                    next[p] += 1;\n                    msg.release();\n                }\n                assert!(cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(|_| false).is_err());\n            });\n        });\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn threaded_mpsc_shared_reference_producers() {\n        // The Sync path: two threads share one &MpscProducer\n        // instead of cloning.\n        const COUNT: u64 = if cfg!(miri) { 100 } else { 50_000 };\n        let mut r = Region::new();\n        let (prod, mut cons) = MpscRing::init(&mut r.0, 64, 4).unwrap().split();\n        let prod = &prod;\n\n        std::thread::scope(|s| {\n            for p in 0..2u64 {\n                s.spawn(move || {\n                    for i in 0..COUNT {\n                        prod.send_with::<Msg>(crate::policy::spin, |m| {\n                            m.seq = i;\n                            m.val = p;\n                        })\n                        .unwrap(); // OK: policy::spin never gives up\n                    }\n                });\n            }\n            s.spawn(move || {\n                let mut next = [0u64; 2];\n                for _ in 0..2 * COUNT {\n                    let msg = cons.reserve_slot_with::<Msg>(crate::policy::spin).unwrap(); // OK: policy::spin never gives up\n                    let p = msg.val as usize;\n                    assert_eq!(msg.seq, next[p]);\n                    next[p] += 1;\n                    msg.release();\n                }\n            });\n        });\n    }\n}"
}
